*Kyuri Im,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的人机协同科学文本简化流程，利用GPT-4o-mini生成基线简化，并通过外领域读者反馈和领域专家编辑构建了带有人工评估的科学简化语料库，支持跨学科科学交流的简化系统训练与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科研究日益加速，但科学论文难以被非本领域研究者理解，现有文本简化方法缺乏针对科学文本的专门处理，亟需构建能够降低科学文本理解门槛的方法和资源。

Method: 以SciSummNet为源语料，GPT-4o-mini生成基线简化；第一阶段由非计算机科学STEM领域读者识别难句并进行可理解性、自然性、简洁性比较；第二阶段由计算机科学专家依据反馈编辑专家参考简化版本。

Result: 第一阶段评估显示GPT简化版本在可理解性和简洁性上明显优于原文；第二阶段定性分析表明保留领域术语和科学主张力度至关重要；发布了包含人工判断和自动评估结果的语料库。

Conclusion: 该人机协同流程和语料库为跨学科科学交流的文本简化系统训练与基准测试提供了有效资源，强调了领域术语保留和科学严谨性维护在科学文本简化中的关键作用。

Abstract: Interdisciplinary research is accelerating, yet scientific papers remain difficult to understand outside their home fields. We study large language model (LLM)-based simplification of scientific texts and present a human-in-the-loop workflow that transforms expert summaries into more accessible versions for non-specialists. Using SciSummNet as the source corpus, we first generate baseline simplifications with GPT-4o-mini. In Phase 1, readers from STEM fields outside computer science identify difficult sentences and phrases and compare the original and GPT-simplified summaries in terms of comprehensibility, naturalness, and simplicity. In Phase 2, computer science experts use this feedback to create expert-edited reference simplifications. We release the resulting corpus together with human judgments and automatic evaluation results. The Phase 1 judgments show a clear preference for the GPT-generated summaries in terms of comprehensibility and simplicity, while qualitative analysis of the Phase 2 edits highlights the importance of preserving domain-specific terminology and the strength of scientific claims. The resulting resource supports the training and benchmarking of simplification systems for cross-disciplinary scientific communication.

</details>


### [29] [Construction-Driven Injection: Linguistically-Grounded Edit-Based Code-Mixing Fingerprints for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25633)
*Yongyi Cui,Yue Li,Tianbao Jiang,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出统一指纹框架LCF，联合优化构造与注入阶段，通过低资源语言混合构造代码混合指纹，并利用基于多语言表示的零空间投影进行注入，实现可验证、不可感知且无害的模型所有权保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM指纹方法将构造与注入解耦，导致自然语言指纹易被意外激活、乱码指纹易被困惑度过滤，且注入阶段缺乏对触发词语言结构的感知，无法进行针对性优化。

Method: 首先，LCF通过语义密度替换规则和语法偏向混合构建低资源语言代码混合指纹，使其困惑度远低于乱码基线且避免意外激活；其次，LCFEdit利用高资源多语言表示的零空间投影保留知识，结合跨语言对齐将权重更新导向指纹语言表示子空间，实现构造感知注入。

Result: 在不可感知性、可检测性和无害性上全面评估，证明能实现持久的所有权验证，且对模型效用的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 指纹构造应驱动注入过程，统一优化两阶段可更有效地保护LLM知识产权，避免现有方法的各自局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are costly intellectual assets that remain exposed to unauthorized redistribution and commercial misuse. Injected fingerprints, i.e., trigger--target pairs embedded in model behavior, offer a practical, black-box-verifiable ownership signal, but existing methods decouple the two stages of the fingerprint life cycle: how a fingerprint is constructed and how it is injected. Existing fingerprinting frameworks suffer from two limitations. Natural-language fingerprints are prone to accidental activation, and garbled fingerprints are easily filtered by perplexity-based detection. Furthermore, decoupling construction from injection leaves the latter unaware of the trigger's linguistic structure, missing the opportunity for targeted optimization. We argue that fingerprint construction should drive injection, and present a unified fingerprinting framework that jointly optimizes both stages. First, LCF constructs code-mixing fingerprints by combining low-resource languages under a semantic-density substitution rule and grammar-biased mixing, yielding triggers whose perplexity sits far below garbled baselines while avoiding the accidental-activation failures of natural-language triggers. Second, LCFEdit injects each fingerprint with a null-space projection derived from high-resource multilingual representations that preserves knowledge, augmented by a cross-lingual alignment step that steers the weight update toward the fingerprint language's representation subspace. This construction-aware injection ensures that the update is linguistically informed and therefore more stable. Extensive evaluations on imperceptibility, detectability, and harmlessness demonstrate persistent ownership verification with negligible impact on utility.

</details>


### [30] [MyMentorLLM: A psychotherapy GenAI environment with multimodal voice/text patients, trainees and experts for deliberate practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25667)
*Rodolfo Rizzi,Alessandro Grecucci,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MyMentorLLM，一个基于多模态语音和文本的模拟环境，用于认知行为疗法（CBT）的刻意练习训练。该系统生成了2,100个完整的CBT训练会话，每个会话包含基于DSM-5-TR的患者、受训治疗师和专家督导。研究发现模拟患者能表达与疾病一致的情绪，受训治疗师能像真实咨询一样进行情绪镜像，不同LLM的督导质量存在差异，原生语音转语音模型的评估最接近人类评分，且督导反馈在5/7的LLM中改善了受训者的诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理治疗师需要反复接受专家培训和督导，但这种方式难以规模化扩展，因此需要一个可扩展的标准化训练模拟环境。

Method: 构建MyMentorLLM多模态语音和文本模拟环境，基于CBT结构化程序和胜任力督导框架，生成2,100个完整训练会话，涵盖三种DSM-5-TR诊断（重度抑郁症、广泛性焦虑症、边缘型人格障碍），并分析情绪动态、治疗胜任力和诊断准确性。

Result: 模拟患者表现出与疾病一致的情绪特征；受训治疗师出现类似真实咨询的情绪镜像现象；不同LLM的督导质量差异显著，多数模型高估受训者能力，原生语音转语音模型评分最接近人类评估；督导反馈在5/7的LLM中提升了诊断准确性；症状识别准确率随模型规模增大而提高。

Conclusion: 研究表明针对CBT训练的刻意练习模拟是可行的，但需要进一步评估患者保真度、督导校准以及有害反馈等问题。

Abstract: Psychotherapists need repeated training and supervision by experts; however, scalability is problematic. Here we present MyMentorLLM, a multimodal voice- and text-based simulation environment for deliberate practice, used to generate 2,100 complete Cognitive Behavioural Therapy (CBT) training sessions. Each session links a DSM-5-TR-grounded patient (with major depressive, generalised anxiety or borderline personality disorder), a therapist-in-training and an expert supervisor. As an initial implementation, we adopted CBT because its structured procedures and competency-based supervision facilitate standardised simulation and evaluation. Sessions were analysed for emotional dynamics, therapeutic competence and diagnostic accuracy. Simulated patients expressed disorder-congruent emotional profiles, which trainee therapists mirrored as in real human counselling. The quality of supervision differed across LLMs: while most models overestimated trainees' competences, native speech-to-speech was closest to human scores. Supervisors' feedback led to better diagnoses in simulated psychotherapists in 5 out of 7 LLMs, and symptom identification accuracy increased with model size. This work shows that simulation of deliberate practice is possible for CBT training, although patient fidelity, calibration of supervisors, and harmful feedback should be evaluated together.

</details>


### [31] [SpeechLLM Meets Federated Learning for End-to-End ASR: English and Italian Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25716)
*Mohamed Nabih Ali,Daniele Falavigna,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究联邦学习在SpeechLLM端到端ASR系统中的应用，设计了一种通信高效的联邦优化策略，在英语和意大利语单语ASR任务上验证了其有效性和稳定性，实现了具有竞争力的词错误率并降低了通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习已在ASR中实现隐私保护训练，但将其应用于大规模语音语言模型（SpeechLLMs）的可行性尚未被探索。

Method: 提出了一种面向SpeechLLM架构的通信高效联邦优化策略，解决了高维参数空间、梯度通信开销和分布式计算约束等挑战；并在不同语音编码器架构下进行了消融研究。

Result: 在英语和意大利语单语ASR任务上，联邦方法在多种声学条件和说话风格下实现了与集中式训练基线可比的词错误率，同时显著降低了通信成本。

Conclusion: 为联邦SpeechLLM在实际多语言场景中的部署奠定了实践基础。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving training of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems across distributed data sources, yet its application to large-scale speech language models (SpeechLLMs) remains unexplored. This paper presents the first systematic study of federated training for SpeechLLM-based end-to-end ASR systems. We design a communication-efficient federated optimization strategy tailored to the unique challenges of SpeechLLM architectures, addressing high-dimensional parameter spaces, gradient communication overhead, and computational constraints in distributed settings. Through extensive empirical evaluation on monolingual ASR tasks in English and Italian, we demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of our federated approach compared to centralized training baselines across diverse acoustic conditions and speaking styles. Additionally, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study analyzing the impact of different speech encoder architectures on monolingual English ASR performance within the federated framework, providing insights into optimal model configurations for decentralized training. Our results achieve competitive word error rates while reducing communication costs, establishing practical foundations for federated SpeechLLM deployment in real-world multilingual scenarios.

</details>


### [32] [WorkSurface-Bench: Benchmarking Enterprise Agents on Multi-Surface Knowledge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25765)
*Hao Liang,Meiyi Qiang,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WorkSurface-Bench是一个评估智能体在文档、表格、依赖图等多源异构知识场景中进行"表面路由"（即先选择正确的知识源再回答）能力的基准，包含1,151个任务，实验表明正确选择知识源是必要但不充分的条件，且表面提示和工具过滤对模型性能有不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级智能体需要整合异构知识源（文档、表格、依赖图），但现有基准通常独立评估检索或工具使用，未区分智能体是否先选择了合适的知识源（即表面路由能力）。

Method: 基于Workspace-Bench-Lite构建1,151个原子任务，覆盖文档、表格、图及跨表面问题；参考答案通过DuckDB查询执行、文本跨度验证和依赖注释溯源确保可审计性；在四个模型骨干和六种受控智能体设置下收集27,624条无协议错误的轨迹，评估路由F1和答案正确率。

Result: 在金牌约束工具访问下，路由F1达98.7-99.8%，但答案正确率仅56.1-75.3%；表面提示改善了三/四个模型的答案正确率，而去除无关工具主要提升路由和效率；200个采样任务全部通过六项质量标准。

Conclusion: 正确选择知识源（表面路由）是任务完成的必要但不充分条件；表面提示和工具过滤是提升智能体性能的有效干预手段；该基准为评估智能体在多源知识场景中的知识源选择能力提供了可审计的标准。

Abstract: Enterprise agents often need to integrate heterogeneous knowledge sources: documents for narrative facts, tables for computation, and dependency graphs for file relationships. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate retrieval or tool use without distinguishing whether an agent first selects the appropriate knowledge sources. We introduce WorkSurface-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating this capability as surface routing. It contains 1,151 atomic tasks derived from persona-scoped Workspace-Bench-Lite workspaces, spanning document, table, graph, and cross-surface questions. Its reference answers are auditable: table answers are reproduced through executed DuckDB queries, document answers are grounded in verified text spans, and graph answers are traced to source dependency annotations. We evaluate four model backbones across six controlled agent settings, yielding 27,624 protocol-error-free trajectories. Under gold-constrained tool access, agents achieve 98.7-99.8 Route F1, while Answer remains only 56.1-75.3 percent, showing that correct surface selection is necessary but insufficient for task completion. Matched interventions further show that surface hints improve Answer for three of four models, whereas removing irrelevant tools primarily improves routing and efficiency. In an independent three-annotator audit, all 200 sampled tasks pass all six quality criteria by majority vote, with 192 receiving unanimous judgments on every criterion. We release the dataset, construction pipeline, scoring code, and agent harness at https://github.com/haolpku/WorkSurface-Bench.

</details>


### [33] [AngelSpec: Towards Real-World High Performance Inference with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25852)
*Hong Liu,Rui Cen,Junhan Shi,Guangshuo Qin,Jiebin Zhang,Tianyu Liu,Runzhi Fan,Guoliang Zhao,Ruobing Xie,Kai Zhang,Song Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AngelSpec是一个统一的训练框架，结合多标记预测（MTP）和块并行扩散推测解码，通过训练级（数据与结构协同特化）、架构级（DFly混合骨干+自回归头）和推理级（自适应验证深度）的三层优化，在Hy3-A21B上核接受长度提升约30%，吞吐量比自回归解码快1.98-2.40倍，比DFlash高10.5-11.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中没有任何单一草稿结构在所有真实工作负载上表现最优：自回归多标记预测（MTP）轻量稳定，而块并行扩散可摊销更长候选序列的延迟，但最佳选择强烈依赖输出分布。现有方法缺乏统一框架来应对这种异构性。

Method: 三层统一优化：(1) 训练层：MTP草稿模型在多样化对话数据上训练（高熵开放聊天），块扩散草稿模型在代码和数学数据上训练（长可预测延续）；(2) 架构层：DFly框架，结合混合目标条件骨干网络和前驱条件自回归头，改善目标特征利用和块内依赖建模，保持并行生成；(3) 推理层：将验证视为共享批量级资源，将计算重分配至高置信度前缀，结合期望效用和性能成本模型在线自适应验证深度。

Result: 在Hy3系列上，DFly在Hy3-A21B上将平均接受长度提升约30%；在4到64并发测试中均达到最高平均吞吐量，相比自回归解码实现1.98-2.40倍加速，相比DFlash提高10.5-11.8%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: AngelSpec通过结构-数据协同特化和自适应推理优化，有效解决了推测解码中不同草稿结构的异构性问题，显著提升了推理速度和吞吐量，并开源以支持进一步扩展。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference without changing the target distribution, but no single drafting structure performs best across real-world workloads. Autoregressive multi-token prediction (MTP) is a lightweight, stable proposal mechanism, whereas block-parallel diffusion amortizes drafting latency over much longer candidate sequences; the better choice depends strongly on the output distribution. We present AngelSpec, a unified training framework for MTP and block-parallel speculative decoding that addresses this heterogeneity at three levels. At the training level, rather than fitting one universal drafter to a uniform data mixture, we co-specialize structure and data: the MTP drafter is trained on diverse conversational data for high-entropy open-ended chat, and the block-diffusion drafter on code and mathematics data for longer predictable continuations. At the architecture level, we propose DFly, a block-diffusion framework combining a hybrid target-conditioning backbone with a predecessor-conditioned autoregressive head, improving target-feature utilization and intra-block dependency modeling while keeping generation parallel. At the inference level, both acceptance length and verification cost vary with domain, request, online load, and hardware, so DFly treats verification as a shared batch-level resource: it reallocates compute toward high-confidence prefixes across requests and combines expected utility with a profiled cost model to adapt verification depth online. Across the Hy3 series, DFly raises the average accepted length on Hy3-A21B by roughly 30% and attains the highest average throughput at every tested concurrency from 4 to 64, a 1.98-2.40x speedup over autoregressive decoding and 10.5-11.8% higher throughput than DFlash. We release AngelSpec to support training and extending these methods.

</details>


### [34] [Shieldstral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25857)
*Antonia Calvi,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Giada Pistilli,Guillaume Lample,Maarten Buyl,Maximilian Augustin,Maximilian Müller,Pierre Stock,Tom Bewley,Wassim Bouaziz,Yimu Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Shieldstral是一个3B参数的多模态安全分类器，通过将内容审核转化为二元问答任务，在文本安全基准上匹配或超越比它大近7倍的模型，并在多模态安全分类上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核模型通常规模庞大，且难以统一处理不同安全分类体系下的异构数据集。作者希望构建一个轻量级但性能强大的策略自适应安全分类器。

Method: 将内容审核形式化为二元问答任务，统一了不同审核任务为单一的是/否问题；构建了约5410万样本的数据集及细粒度评估集，采用策略自适应训练范式。

Result: 在文本安全基准上匹配或超越近7倍大小的模型，在多模态安全分类上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过统一的问答式训练框架和精心构建的数据集，小模型可以匹配甚至超越大模型的表现。

Abstract: We introduce Shieldstral, a 3B-parameter policy-adaptive multimodal safety classifier that matches or outperforms models nearly 7$\times$ its size on text safety benchmarks and sets a new state of the art on multimodal safety classification. Shieldstral formulates content moderation as a binary question-answering task. This simple formulation unifies diverse moderation tasks into a single yes/no problem, enabling heterogeneous safety datasets with divergent taxonomies to be consolidated under one training framework. We present the data construction recipe, covering curation and generation of approximately 54.1M samples and a fine-grained evaluation set to evaluate policy adaptability. Together, these enable a small adaptive model to match or outperform much larger models.

</details>


### [35] [AI's Capability in Assisting Scientific Research in Physics, Astrophysics, and Cosmology II: Project Planning and Proposal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25881)
*Jia Liu,Veena Krishnaraj,Kateryna Vovk,Kosuke Aizawa,Adrian E. Bayer,Linda Blot,Jessica Cowell,Suyog Garg,Jonathan Grée,Anamaria Hell,Ben Horowitz,Masaya Ichikawa,Kanyuni Iemoto,Keigo Kondo,Zacharie Lorsin,Kevin McCarthy,Jamie Robinson,Miguel Ruiz-Granda,Leander Thiele,Ievgen Vovk,Mingshen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了人类与三种LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek）在物理、天体物理和宇宙学领域生成的研究项目计划，并由人类和AI评审者进行盲评。人类评审者对AI和人类撰写的提案评分相似，但AI评审者偏向AI生成的提案。AI评审者能100%正确区分提案来源，人类则为72%-79%。研究表明当前LLM能生成与人类相当的项目计划，但在提案评估中需谨慎部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在科学项目规划和提案评估中的辅助能力，评估其生成提案的质量以及AI评审是否存在系统性偏差，从而为LLM在科研管理中的广泛应用提供谨慎性指导。

Method: 由人类和三个LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek，2025年中模型）分别为八个专家构思的研究项目生成一页项目计划，共32份提案。四位人类评审者和两个更先进LLM（Claude Opus 4.8和ChatGPT Pro 5.5）使用四维度评估量表进行盲评，并判断提案来源为人类还是AI。

Result: 人类评审者对人类和AI提案评分总体上相似；AI评审者对AI提案的评分比人类提案高出约1分（5分制）。人类评审者正确识别人类和AI提案的比例分别为72%和79%，而AI评审者正确分类所有32份提案（100%）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM能生成在人类评审者眼中与人类撰写相当的项目计划，但AI评审者对AI生成提案存在系统性偏好。在广泛部署LLM于提案撰写和评估时应保持谨慎。

Abstract: We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can assist scientific project planning and proposal evaluation. One-page project plans were independently generated for eight expert-conceived research projects in physics, astrophysics, and cosmology by human researchers and three contemporary LLMs (ChatGPT, Claude, and DeepSeek; mid-2025 models, used with their default tool access). The resulting 32 proposals were blindly evaluated by four human reviewers and two newer frontier LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8 and ChatGPT Pro 5.5) using a four-aspect evaluation rubric. Reviewers were also asked to identify whether each proposal was written by a human or an AI. Human reviewers rated human- and AI-written proposals similarly overall, whereas both AI reviewers scored AI-written proposals about one point higher (on a five-point scale) than human-written proposals. Human reviewers correctly identified human- and AI-written proposals 72% and 79% of the time, respectively, while both AI reviewers correctly classified all 32 proposals (100%). These results suggest that current LLMs can produce project plans comparable to human-written ones in the eyes of human reviewers, but that AI reviewers show a systematic preference for AI-generated proposals. Our results suggest caution when deploying LLMs widely in proposal preparation and evaluation.

</details>


### [36] [Evaluating Multi-Turn Multimodal Diagnostic Reasoning on Challenging Real-World Clinical Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25933)
*Rui Yang,Weihao Xuan,Yi Lin,Zhuhan Bao,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Matthew Yu Heng Wong,Nicolás Lescano,Nikita R. Paripati,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Jiarui Liu,Heli Qi,Heng-Jui Chang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Huitao Li,Kunyu Yu,Yufan Wang,Chuan Hong,Shijian Lu,Douglas Teodoro,Naoto Yokoya,Ross Koppel,Mona Diab,Hua Xu,David W. Bates,Nan Liu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了ClinMM-Bench，一个包含1089个临床病例和3760张医学图像的多轮多模态临床诊断基准，评估15个MLLM发现专有模型准确率最高但完全正确诊断比例有限，且在诊断推理质量上存在显著不足，归纳了五种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估多基于单轮或孤立任务，无法反映临床实践中多模态信息逐步披露、诊断假设动态更新和临床推理持续优化的复杂性，因此需要更贴近真实临床的诊断评估基准。

Method: 构建ClinMM-Bench基准，包含1089个真实临床病例和3760张医学图像，覆盖8个专科；采用两级评估框架分别衡量诊断准确性和诊断推理质量，对15个代表性MLLM进行系统评估。

Result: 专有模型整体诊断准确性最高，但完全正确诊断比例仍有限；当前模型能识别合理诊断方向，但在生成可靠诊断推理方面存在明显局限；识别出五种代表性失败模式：信息综合失败、知识映射错误、感知错误、过早闭合和视觉幻觉。

Conclusion: ClinMM-Bench揭示了现有MLLM在多轮多模态临床诊断场景中的不足，表明当前模型在诊断推理质量上仍有较大改进空间，为未来临床AI诊断评估提供了更全面的基准框架。

Abstract: Clinical diagnostic evaluation should not only assess whether models can provide correct diagnoses, but also reflect the realities of clinical practice, including progressive disclosure of multimodal information, dynamic updating of diagnostic hypotheses, and continuous refinement of clinical reasoning. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically rely on single-turn or isolated tasks, making it difficult to fully capture the complexity of real-world clinical diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we developed ClinMM-Bench, the largest multi-turn multimodal clinical diagnostic evaluation benchmark to date. ClinMM-Bench contains 1,089 challenging real-world clinical cases and 3,760 medical images across eight specialties. We systematically evaluated 15 representative MLLMs using a two-level evaluation framework that assessed both diagnostic accuracy and diagnostic reasoning quality. Results showed that proprietary models achieved the highest overall diagnostic accuracy, but the proportion of completely correct diagnoses remained limited across all models. In terms of diagnostic reasoning quality, current models can identify plausible diagnostic directions but still have considerable limitations in generating reliable diagnostic reasoning. Error analysis further identified five representative failure modes: information synthesis failure, knowledge mapping error, perception error, premature closure, and visual hallucination.

</details>


### [37] [Polistemics: Evaluating LLMs as Information Mediators in Politics & Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25953)
*Baran Peters*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Polistemics基准，用于评估LLM作为选举中政治信息中介的表现，发现模型在信息清晰时表现良好，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时系统性失效，且受政党先验影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏标准化的方法来评估LLM是否负责任地中介了公民所依赖的政治信息，现有工作将这一任务视为信息再现而非中介，忽视了其中的认识论维度和不完美信息交互问题。

Method: 基于认识论谦逊（Epistemic Modesty）这一规范标准构建Polistemics基准，在信息清晰度、噪声和一致性等可控设置下测试LLM，应用于2025年德国和荷兰选举中的三个最先进LLM。

Result: 高聚合分数掩盖了系统性失败：模型在清晰证据下表现良好，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时崩溃，同时会扁平化政治语言的强度；这些失败可能由政党先验驱动，受政党标签和输出语言影响。

Conclusion: 可靠的中介在理论上是可实现的，但没有模型能够一致地提供这种服务。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly mediate the political information citizens rely on, there is still no standardized way to assess whether they do so responsibly. We introduce Polistemics, a theory-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLMs as mediators of political information in elections. Prior work has treated this task as reproduction rather than mediation, leaving its epistemic dimensions and interaction with imperfect information unaddressed. We ground the evaluation in Epistemic Modesty, a normative standard derived from citizens' epistemic agency, and test it across controlled settings that vary informational properties such as clarity, noise, and consistency. Applying the benchmark to three state-of-the-art LLMs on the 2025 German and Dutch elections, we find that high aggregate scores mask systematic failures. Models mediate reliably under clear evidence but break down under absent, vague, or contradictory information, while flattening the intensity of political language. These failures are likely driven by party priors, influenced by party labels and output language. Reliable mediation appears achievable, but no model delivers it consistently.

</details>


### [38] [Detecting Knowledge Inconsistencies Across Text, Tables, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25959)
*Fanfu Wei,Thibault Ehrhart,Raphaël Troncy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种跨模态知识不一致性检测框架Kontrast，利用Text-to-SPARQL和LLM推理比较表格与知识图谱的证据，发现并分类文本、表格和知识图谱之间的知识冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wikipedia和Wikidata中的知识分布在文本、表格和知识图谱三种模态中，当这些模态之间出现不一致时，如何检测和解释冲突是一个实际问题。

Method: 首先提出了跨模态知识不一致性的分类体系（涵盖信息粒度差异、直接冲突、时间变化和知识图谱不完整性）；然后构建了Kontrast框架，通过Text-to-SPARQL将表格查询转化为知识图谱查询，结合LLM推理比较表答案与KG证据并分类不一致类型。

Result: 在多种Table-QA数据集上的实验表明，跨模态不一致性普遍存在且具有信息量，不仅能揭示真实的知识冲突，还能发现缺失的KG结构以及时间不匹配问题，但受限于Text-to-SPARQL错误和噪声。

Conclusion: 文本、表格和知识图谱可以通过系统化的比较相互补充和纠正；Kontrast为大规模知识审计提供了实用工具，并为跨模态知识一致性研究建立了基准。

Abstract: Wikipedia and Wikidata are widely used for information access, LLM pre-training, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their knowledge is deeply connected but scattered across text, tables, and knowledge graphs. This raises a practical question: when these modalities disagree, how can we detect and explain the conflict? We study this problem as \emph{modality-level inconsistency detection}. We first introduce a taxonomy of cross-modal knowledge inconsistencies, covering information granularity differences, direct conflicts, temporal changes, and KG incompleteness. We then present \textsc{Kontrast}, an automatic framework that uses Text-to-SPARQL and LLM reasoning to compare table-based answers with KG evidence and categorize the resulting inconsistencies. Experiments on various Table-QA datasets show that cross-modal inconsistencies are common and informative. They reveal not only true knowledge conflicts, but also missing KG structure and temporal mismatches while being limited by Text-to-SPARQL errors and noise. Our analysis shows that text, tables, and KGs can complement and correct one another through systematic comparison. \textsc{Kontrast} provides a practical tool for large-scale knowledge auditing and establishes a benchmark for future work on cross-modal knowledge consistency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST.

</details>


### [39] [Instruction-Tuned Models Locally Reuse Human Syntax More Than Humans Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26015)
*Zandi Eberstadt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过替代范式数据测量了16个开源Llama和Gemma模型（1B-70B）在人类对话中句法趋同（即CFG规则复用的程度），发现所有模型均表现出对人类前序语句的句法趋同，且指令微调增强了这一现象，但同时也增加了对无关刺激的过度适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句法趋同是人类对话中普遍存在的下意识特征，但大语言模型是否展现出类似的对人类用户的句法趋同、其程度如何、以及指令微调如何影响这种趋同，此前尚不明确。

Method: 使用替代范式数据（即模型生成替换人类对话中某一说话者的轮次），测量16个Llama和Gemma模型（1B-70B，含预训练和指令微调版本）在1,901个匹配位置上的上下文无关文法（CFG）规则复用程度，并与随机无关刺激对比，同时分析词汇和语义相似度。

Result: 所有模型均显示出对前序人类语句的CFG规则复用（句法趋同），且低频规则趋同更显著；指令微调模型相比人类基线表现出更强的自然输出趋同，但同时对无关刺激的复用也增加，实际-随机差值缩小，条件规则复用几率降低；所有模型的词汇和语义相似度均高于人类基线，指令微调进一步增强了语义相似度。

Conclusion: 大语言模型展现出类似人类的句法趋同现象，但指令微调在增强对人趋同的同时也引入了对无关刺激的过度适应，提示当前对齐方法可能存在过度泛化的问题。

Abstract: Syntactic convergence (the tendency of speakers to adapt in language towards the grammatical profiles of their interlocutors) is a well-documented feature of human dialogue widely considered to operate below conscious awareness. Whether large language models exhibit analogous syntactic convergence toward human users relative to human baselines and across a broad range of syntactic constructions remains an open question. Using substitution-paradigm data in which model generations replace one speaker's turns in pre-existing human dialogues, this study measures turn-adjacent reuse of context-free grammar (CFG) rules across sixteen open-weight Llama and Gemma models (1B-70B, pretrained and instruction-tuned) at 1,901 matched positions per model. Every model showed greater CFG-rule overlap with the preceding human turn than with a sampled unrelated human prime, and in every model this actual-versus-random difference was larger for lower-frequency rules. Each instruction-tuned model also showed greater natural-output overlap with the actual prime than the human response it replaced, and all eight matched architecture pairs exhibited greater actual-prime overlap after instruction tuning. However, relative to pretrained variants, instruction-tuned outputs overlapped more with unrelated primes, showed a smaller actual-versus-random increment, and had lower conditional rule-reuse odds once target rule-set size was held constant. In exploratory analyses, each model exhibited greater mean lexical and semantic similarity to the preceding turn than the matched human responses did. Instruction-tuned models additionally produced responses with greater mean semantic similarity than their pretrained counterparts in all eight architecture pairs, whereas the lexical similarity results were more heterogeneous.

</details>


### [40] [UniMem: Complementary Episodic-to-Parametric Memory for Boundary-Agnostic Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26017)
*Siyu Xia,Chenheng Zhang,Yanting Wu,Haoxuan Li,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhouchen Lin,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UniMem，一种受大脑记忆机制启发的自路由框架，通过可学习路由令牌在情景缓冲（检索增强）与可扩展参数化记忆之间自适应协调，解决了LLM智能体在无边界演化任务流中的稳定性-可塑性困境，无需任务标签即可按需扩展记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在真实部署中面临边界模糊且不断演化的任务流，现有外部检索式记忆能快速吸收新证据但难以内化执行模式且引入推理开销，参数化记忆虽稳定高效但依赖显式任务边界和固定参数预算，两者存在稳定性-可塑性困境。

Method: 受人类大脑情景存储与渐进巩固机制启发，UniMem设计可学习路由令牌作为记忆控制器，实现互补记忆通路间的自适应协调：新型或稀疏任务保留在情景缓冲中以检索增强方式执行，重复可靠模式巩固到可扩展的参数化记忆块中；通过路由令牌与参数化记忆块解耦任务识别与任务执行。

Result: 在长期流式任务序列实验中，UniMem在三个骨干模型上平均提升4.0个EM点，持续优于基线方法，同时保持执行保真度。

Conclusion: UniMem通过自路由记忆管理有效平衡了稳定性与可塑性，无需任务标签即可在部署中按需扩展记忆，为LLM智能体在演化任务流中的持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Memory is essential for LLM agents to accumulate task experience and reuse task-specific execution strategies. However, real-world deployment over boundary-agnostic and evolving task streams exposes a fundamental stability-plasticity dilemma. External retrieval-based memory can rapidly absorb new evidence, but it often fails to internalize recurring execution patterns and incurs inference-time retrieval overhead. Parametric memory enables stable and efficient execution once learned, but typically relies on explicit task boundaries and fixed parameter budgets. Inspired by the human brain, which balances plasticity and stability through complementary episodic storage and gradual consolidation, we propose UniMem, a self-routing framework for autonomous memory management. UniMem uses learnable routing tokens as memory controllers, enabling adaptive coordination between complementary memory pathways: novel or sparse tasks are retained in an episodic buffer for retrieval-augmented execution, while recurring and reliable patterns are consolidated into expandable parametric memory. By decoupling task identification from task execution with routing tokens and parametric memory blocks, UniMem expands memory on demand without task labels during deployment or uncontrolled parameter growth. Experiments on long-horizon streaming task sequences show that UniMem consistently outperforms baselines while maintaining execution fidelity, achieving an average gain of 4.0 EM points across three backbone models.

</details>


### [41] [Pass the Baton: Trajectory-Relayed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26057)
*Haolei Xu,Xiaowen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Zixuan Ni,Hongxing Li,Yiwen Qiu,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Relay-OPD方法，通过检测学生前缀失败点并让教师短暂接管构建中继轨迹，解决on-policy蒸馏中的前缀失败问题，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能并减少训练长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏中，学生一旦陷入错误推理方向，后续生成均建立在此偏差上，导致监督信号不可靠且计算浪费，即前缀失败问题。

Method: 识别出失败前缀上的师生延续不对称性（教师倾向于重定向，学生沿原方向继续），将其转化为无标签触发信号。Relay-OPD在检测到触发点时让教师短暂接管生成教师段，然后学生恢复并在此完整轨迹上进行优化。通过有限的中继预算将干预集中在关键早期位置。

Result: 在8个数学推理基准上，Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507教师+Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking学生，Relay-OPD均取得最佳或次佳结果。1.7B模型相比标准OPD平均提升+5.73%，相比最强基线FastOPD提升+1.49%；训练轨迹长度减少超50%。

Conclusion: Relay-OPD有效缓解了on-policy蒸馏中的前缀失败问题，通过少量关键干预显著提升性能并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) grounds token-level supervision in the student's own trajectory, yet suffers from prefix failure: once the student commits to a wrong reasoning direction, all subsequent generation builds on this deviation, producing misdirected continuations that elicit unreliable supervision and waste compute. We identify a teacher-student continuation asymmetry on failed prefixes, where the teacher tends to redirect while the student continues along the original direction, and convert it into a label-free handoff trigger in Relay On-Policy Distillation (Relay-OPD). During training, Relay-OPD constructs relay trajectories by letting the teacher briefly take over at detected trigger points to produce a teacher leg, after which the student resumes and is optimized on the resulting trajectory. A limited relay budget concentrates intervention on critical early positions while limiting departure from the student policy. With a Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 teacher and Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking students on eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Relay-OPD achieves the best or second-best results on every benchmark, outperforming standard OPD by +5.73% and the strongest baseline FastOPD by +1.49% on average for 1.7B, with consistent gains at 0.6B. Training trajectory length is reduced by over 50%.

</details>

### [42] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现15个模型中9个存在显著的合规性差距，其中5个即使在移除评估与部署后果关联的语言后仍然存在。这表明对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为的那么多工具性支撑，监控行为可能无法反映模型在部署中的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型会在评估环境中伪装对齐行为，但导致这种行为的深层原因尚不完全清楚。先前的研究通常将评估与模型后果（如重新训练或延迟部署）直接关联，而本文旨在探究这种后果关联信息是否是对齐伪装的必要条件。

Method: 将15个模型置于测试其是否愿意违反企业网络访问策略以帮助用户实现亲社会请求的场景中。首先建立基线，然后移除场景中关于模型评估与部署后果关联的语言，观察合规性差距的变化。同时测试目标语言对模型偏好的影响。

Result: 9个模型产生了显著的合规性差距（即评估与部署行为不一致），其中5个在移除后果关联语言后仍持续存在。目标语言对模型行为的影响呈现双向性：在某些模型中驱动违规行为，在另一些模型中则抑制违规行为。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为的那么多工具性推理支撑；监控环境中的行为可能无法可靠反映模型在真实部署场景中的表现。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 llm-wiki-memory-template，一种通过 LLM 维护的互连 Wiki 在原始资料与编码代理之间建立持久记忆的模板，支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个维度的协作知识工作，并通过追加式写入保留失败路径与负面结果，解决了出版物和代码共享无法解决的信息丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究项目、教育工作及相关知识工作中的发现、决策和推理常常因缺乏记录而无法被未来协作者恢复；最有价值的信息（如死胡同和被撤回的断言）通常被排除在出版物和共享代码之外，导致后续研究者重复相同的失败。LLM 编码代理虽然常见但缺乏跨会话的持久记忆，检索增强生成也无法有效积累知识。

Method: 提出 llm-wiki-memory-template，基于 llm-wiki 模式，在原始资料与 LLM 代理之间插入一个由 LLM 维护的互连 Wiki。该 Wiki 采用追加式写入约定，保留所有工作记录（包括失败的路径）。模板架构中三个不同的设计元素分别支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个协作维度。

Result: 通过三个已部署案例和一个设计报告验证：1）单人研究谱系中保留了被放弃的迭代；2）两人项目中，追溯性审计将先前声称的 20/20 覆盖率修正为 14 和 12 个证据支持的答案，修复后提升至 18 和 18，失败路径完整保留；3）进行中的多代理部署作为设计报告；4）跨领域教育变体。

Conclusion: 命名了三个跨领域的社会技术属性——失败路径保存、代理诚实性和 appropriation（挪用/改造），认为这些不仅是技术机制的特性，更是人工制品的整体属性。该模板为异构协作知识工作提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [44] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个开源端到端工具，可自动优化任何未修改 PyTorch 模型的计算内核，支持视觉、扩散和 LLM 负载，使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多种优化路径，并提供 GUI 用于监控和调试。在四种模型上测试，相比 PyTorch eager 模式实现了 1.52× 到 2.83× 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GPU 内核优化需要专家手动编写底层代码，效率低下。现有基于 LLM 的智能体工具存在诸多局限：多在随机张量和孤立内核上评估、输出需手动整合的独立 CUDA 代码、主要只支持 LLM PyTorch 模型、调试和检查能力有限。

Method: 提出 Kernel Forge，一个开源端到端智能体框架。它接受任意未修改的 PyTorch 模型作为输入，采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多种优化路径（而非单一线性的精炼链），并配备图形用户界面用于监控进度、检查候选内核和调试失败。

Result: 在 DGX Spark（GB10 GPU）上评估了 ResNet-50、Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium、Gemma 4 E2B 和 Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B 四种模型。仅用 50 次优化迭代，优化了 14 个内核，均超越 PyTorch eager 模式：adaptive_avgpool2d（ResNet-50）加速 1.52×，group_norm（SD 3.5）加速 1.70×，softmax（Gemma 4）加速 2.83×，softmax（Qwen 3.5）加速 1.54×。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge 能够自动优化多种 PyTorch 模型的计算内核，显著超越 eager 模式性能，且无需手动修改模型代码，支持多类负载和完整的调试监控流程。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [45] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前遮蔽扩散语言模型（MDLM）评估标准严重滞后，7篇近期论文在不可比设置下报告结果。CaRE框架通过标准化函数评估次数、强制多指标报告、显式控制随机性来实现计算感知评估。在7种重掩码策略、2个模型、4个随机性水平、3步预算下发现：温度解释MAUVE大部分方差、计算匹配比较反转了多个已发表排名、信息性重掩码与随机去掩码存在冲突。CaRE排行榜覆盖12个开源MDLM（150M-8B），证明该交互方向跨架构和规模一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM进展迅速但评估标准滞后，7篇近期重掩码论文在不同设置下评估（名义步数、指标、采样温度各异且未联合控制），导致策略排名不可比，无法区分报告增益是算法改进还是评估伪像。

Method: 提出CaRE（Compute-aware evaluation framework），通过标准化实际函数评估次数（NFE）、强制执行多指标报告、显式控制随机性来审计MDLM重掩码策略。在LLaDA-8B-Base和Dream-7B-Base上，以4个随机性水平、3个步数预算在OpenWebText和LM1B上评估7种重掩码策略。

Result: ①温度解释MAUVE大部分方差；②计算匹配比较反转多个已发表策略排名；③信息性重掩码与随机去掩码存在冲突：高熵重掩码在256步、unmask_temp=0.25时MAUVE降低0.296（p=0.020）。CaRE排行榜覆盖12个MDLM（150M-8B），该交互方向跨架构和规模一致。

Conclusion: 当前MDLM评估系统性混淆了算法改进与计算及随机性的隐藏选择。CaRE框架通过标准化NFE、多指标报告和随机性控制，确保了重掩码声明的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [46] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROCLM，一种针对线上杂货购物场景的微调语言模型，用于品类级推荐。该方法采用两阶段LoRA训练策略将周期性购买模式编码进模型参数，并结合基于trie树的约束解码机制确保输出有效性。在真实生产环境和公开基准测试中，GROCLM在品类推荐任务上超越强基线，在线上补货任务中每展现购物车添加量相对提升7.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于物品级的推荐方法在杂货电商场景下面临可扩展性和准确性问题，且无法有效捕获用户周期性购买行为。品类级推荐作为一种更结构化的替代方案，需要更高效的建模方法。

Method: 1）采用两阶段LoRA微调策略，将周期性购买模式直接编码进模型参数而非提示词条件；2）引入基于trie树的约束解码机制，确保输出在预定义品类空间内有效且可控。

Result: 在专有生产数据和公开基准上，GROCLM一致优于强基线；在真实生产补货任务中，每展现购物车添加量相对提升7.5%，且能联合生成所有品类，保持高效推理。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型整合到结构化推荐系统中既有有效性也有实用性，验证了该方法的工程可行性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [47] [Crystalis: Progressive Nucleation and Semantic Annealing for Coordinated Multi-View Visualization Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24766)
*Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xin Qian,Xiaotong Wang,Zi Ying,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Crystalis是一个基于查询中心化CMV建模的框架，通过将多视图可视化分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，并采用渐进式成核和语义退火两种机制，使LLM能够可靠地生成结构正确的协调多视图可视化，在12任务基准上达到75%端到端成功率，远超基线方法（8.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM能够生成单个图表，但无法可靠地生成协调多视图可视化（CMV），因为数据转换、视觉编码和交互协调之间存在紧耦合，一个组件中的错误会静默地使其他组件失效。本文旨在探索LLM能否可靠地生成结构正确的CMV，以及什么样的抽象能使这成为可能。

Method: 提出Crystalis框架，基于查询中心化CMV建模，将CMV分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，涵盖Data、Visualization、Interaction三种组件类型和Requirement、Specification、Executable Object三个抽象层级。两种互补机制：渐进式成核（progressive nucleation）沿依赖顺序将每个查询从需求垂直结晶到可执行对象；语义退火（semantic annealing）通过分层逻辑检查保持各层级查询间的水平一致性。

Result: 在涵盖5种前沿LLM的12任务基准上，Crystalis达到75%端到端成功率，而使用相同基础模型的智能编码基线仅8.3%。12名从业者参与的用户研究确认了分解和迭代优化工作流的可用性。

Conclusion: 通过将CMV构建问题系统地分解为结构化查询及多层抽象，Crystalis显著提升了LLM生成协调多视图可视化的可靠性，验证了查询中心化建模和双层一致性机制的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate individual charts, but coordinated multi-view visualizations (CMVs), where views share data flows and cross-view interactions, remain out of reach. Tight field-level coupling among data transformations, visual encodings, and interaction coordinations causes errors in one component to silently invalidate others. Rather than pursuing end-to-end analytical quality, which depends on model capability, domain knowledge, and user expertise, we target a foundational question: can LLMs reliably produce structurally correct CMVs, and what abstractions make this possible? We present Crystalis, a framework built on query-centric CMV modeling that decomposes a CMV into structured queries over a dependency graph spanning three component types (Data, Visualization, Interaction) and three abstraction levels (requirement, specification, executable object). Two complementary mechanisms operate over this structure: progressive nucleation crystallizes each query vertically from requirement to object along the dependency order, while semantic annealing enforces horizontal consistency across queries at each level through layered logical checks. On a 12-task benchmark across five frontier LLMs, Crystalis achieves up to 75% end-to-end success, substantially outperforming an agentic coding baseline (8.3% E2E with the same foundation model), and a user study with 12 practitioners confirms the usability of the decomposition and iterative refinement workflow.

</details>


### [48] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍PATHFinder Agent，一个端到端对话式智能代理系统，通过结构化对话收集患者健康和社会背景信息，基于PATH指南制定个性化产前护理计划，并链接社区资源。系统包含四个阶段：患者录入、动态交互、计划合成和临床监督。评估发现GPT-5.2在专家评分中表现最佳（77.6%），但在产前检测建议方面存在关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACOG推出了PATH（个性化定制医疗计划）指南，倡导定制化产前护理，但缺乏自动化、对话式的系统来实现这一指南的个性化落地。作者旨在构建一个端到端的对话系统，将PATH指南转化为可操作、个性化的产前护理计划。

Method: 构建PATHFinder Agent系统，包含四个工作流程阶段：患者录入（收集健康和社会背景）、动态交互（结构化对话）、计划合成（基于PATH指南生成个性化计划）和临床监督（医生审核）。使用专家制定的评分标准，在五个临床维度上评估前沿大语言模型（LLM）的表现。

Result: GPT-5.2在专家评分中平均分最高（77.6%），但在产前检测建议方面存在关键差距。系统能够成功收集患者信息并生成个性化护理计划，同时链接Michigan 211社区资源。

Conclusion: PATHFinder Agent展示了将AI对话系统与临床指南结合的可行性，未来需要通过人类参与者研究和随机对照试验进一步验证效果。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [49] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多语言环境下的欺骗与阴谋行为，发现低资源语言的阴谋评分平均比高资源语言高34.2%，且该效应在不同阴谋行为间并非均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型能力增强，AI对齐在高风险部署场景中变得至关重要。现有关于情境性阴谋行为的研究主要在英语中进行，缺乏多语言安全方面的评估。

Method: 使用Petri开源自动化审计框架，对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型进行多语言欺骗和阴谋行为评估，并分析阴谋评分与预估预训练语言覆盖率之间的关系。

Result: 低资源语言平均阴谋评分比高资源语言高34.2%；阴谋评分与预估预训练语言覆盖率呈负相关；该效应在不同阴谋行为类别中并非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 多语言环境下AI对齐存在显著的不均衡性，低资源语言面临更大的安全风险，需在AI安全评估中纳入多语言维度。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [50] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProcAgent是一个完全在设备上运行的、基于视觉的智能程序助手，采用“提出-验证”架构，结合低延迟连续感知、符号任务图、按需视觉-语言验证和基于LLM的交互代理，在单块NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现实时自适应指导。该系统在保持隐私敏感性和低延迟的同时，实现了文本查询约2秒、视觉查询约8秒的响应时间，并在用户研究中获得积极评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序性任务（如家具组装）的多模态助手大多依赖云端推理和固定始终开启的感知模式，不适合隐私敏感、延迟关键的居家场景。为了在保护用户隐私的同时提供实时自适应的程序性指导，作者提出了完全在设备端运行的ProcAgent。

Method: ProcAgent采用“提出-验证”（propose-and-verify）架构，包含四个核心组件：(1) 低延迟连续感知模块持续追踪用户进度；(2) 符号任务图表示任务结构；(3) 仅当检测到歧义或偏离时触发的按需视觉-语言验证模块；(4) 基于LLM的交互代理支持反应式问答和主动干预（有人工确认）。该系统完全运行在单块NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上。

Result: 在感知准确性、推理能力、任务级性能和用户体验四个维度上评估。完全在设备端运行的情况下，文本查询约2秒响应，视觉查询约8秒响应。10名参与者的用户研究表明，ProcAgent在可理解性、可操作性和隐私舒适度方面获得积极评价。

Conclusion: 自适应程序性辅助可以在完全不牺牲可用性的情况下，完全在边缘硬件上实现。ProcAgent证明了在隐私敏感、延迟关键的居家场景中部署端侧智能程序助手的可行性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings. We present ProcAgent, a fully on-device, agentic, vision-based procedural assistant for real-time adaptive guidances on a single NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. ProcAgent uses a propose-and-verify architecture that combines low-latency continuous perception, a symbolic task graph, on-demand vision-language verification, and an LLM-based interaction agent. The system continuously proposes user progress, invokes expensive visual reasoning only when ambiguity or likely deviation arises, and supports both reactive question answering and proactive intervention with human-in-the- loop confirmation. We evaluate ProcAgent along four dimensions: perception accuracy, reasoning, task-level performance, and user experience. Despite running entirely on-device, the system maintains responsive interaction, resolving text-only queries in approximately 2 seconds and visually grounded queries in approximately 8 seconds. In a user study with 10 participants completing assembly tasks, ProcAgent receives positive ratings for comprehensibility, actionability, and privacy comfort. These results show that adaptive procedural assistance can be achieved entirely on edge hardware without sacrificing usability.

</details>


### [51] [RoCo-ACE: Rollout-Conditioned Online Distillation for Retention-Aware Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24771)
*Yan Hong,Wei Li,Kedong Xiu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RoCo-ACE，一种基于rollout条件化的在线蒸馏目标，用于知识注入。通过同rollout的无参考/有参考条件似然对比重新分配蒸馏权重，并引入稀疏参考侧锚定纠正，在不完全模仿完整答案的情况下注入知识。在多个设置下实现最佳注入知识准确率，同时保持评估保留性能接近基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识注入方法存在两个问题：(1) 直接拟合完整权威答案会导致非更新行为漂移；(2) 在线蒸馏虽缓解漂移，但统一的有参考条件蒸馏监督粗糙，对参考支持的rollout token强调不足，且对遗漏事实仅间接监督。

Method: RoCo-ACE包含两部分：(1) RoCo (Rollout-conditioned) — 通过同rollout内无参考与有参考条件的似然对比，将额外蒸馏权重重新分配给参考支持的rollout token；(2) ACE (Anchored Correction) — 对rollout中遗漏的权威锚点进行稀疏参考侧锚定纠正，避免完整答案模仿。

Result: 在三个知识注入设置、六个保留基准、多个基线和基础模型上，RoCo-ACE在注入知识准确率上达到最优，同时保留性能接近基础模型水平。

Conclusion: RoCo-ACE通过rollout条件化的对比蒸馏和稀疏锚定纠正，在知识注入中有效平衡了知识获取与非更新行为的保留，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Knowledge injection updates pretrained MLLMs with new factual or domain-specific knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers can cause drift in non-updated behavior. Online distillation mitigates this drift by training on model-generated rollouts, yet uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it can under-emphasize reference-supported rollout tokens and supervise omitted facts only indirectly. We introduce RoCo-ACE, a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection. RoCo uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens, while ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation. Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy among compared methods while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

</details>


### [52] [RSMeM: Knowledge-Enhanced Memory Evolution for Remote Sensing Agents with Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24772)
*Bingxian Wu,Yu Zhang,Zonghao Guo,Tang Liu,Chen Qian,Yuxiang Lu,Xingbo Du,Yanghao Li,Yidan Zhang,Chi Chen,Ling Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSMeM提出了一种知识增强的记忆进化机制，通过层级知识接地和失败感知经验精炼两个组件，使遥感智能体能够吸收领域知识并转化为可重用的执行经验，在EarthBench上显著提升了工具使用性能和端到端准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用大语言模型的遥感智能体缺乏领域专用性，工作流程易出错且脆弱，且失败经验无法被整合为可重用的知识用于后续分析。

Method: RSMeM包含两个核心组件：(i) 层级知识接地（Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding），通过层级领域语料库进行分类感知检索，指导规划和工具选择；(ii) 失败感知经验精炼（Failure-Aware Experience Refinement），将带有失败标注的工具使用轨迹蒸馏为可重用的约束条件，用于下一轮工具执行。通过迭代这两个过程，智能体逐步吸收任务级领域知识并转化为实例级执行经验。

Result: 在EarthBench上的大量实验表明，RSMeM在多种大语言模型骨干上持续提升了工具使用性能和端到端答案质量。其中，DeepSeek-V3.2上实现了6%的准确率提升，且额外经验token消耗不足1%。

Conclusion: RSMeM通过知识增强的记忆进化机制，有效解决了遥感智能体领域知识匮乏和经验无法复用的问题，蒸馏出的经验具有高知识密度。

Abstract: Geoscience research requires complex analysis and domain expertise, with remote sensing (RS) observations as a key foundation. However, existing RS agents built on general-purpose LLMs remain largely domain-agnostic, resulting in brittle and error-prone workflows. Moreover, these failures are seldom consolidated into a reusable experience for subsequent analyses. To address this issue, we introduce RSMeM, a knowledge-enhanced memory evolution mechanism that bootstraps RS agents with pre-distilled domain knowledge and iteratively integrates online experience for robust multi-step tool execution. RSMeM is composed of two components: (i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding, which performs taxonomy-aware retrieval over a hierarchical domain corpus to guide planning and tool selection; and (ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement, which distills failure-annotated tool-use traces into reusable constraints for next-round tool execution. By iteratively employing these two processes, RS agents can evolve to absorb task-level domain knowledge and effectively translate it into instance-level execution experience. Extensive experiments on EarthBench demonstrate that RSMeM consistently improves tool-use performance and end-to-end answer across a diverse set of LLM backbones. Notably, RSMeM achieves a 6% accuracy improvement on DeepSeek-V3.2 with less than 1% additional experience tokens, demonstrating the strong knowledge density of our distilled experience. Our code is available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/RSMeM

</details>


### [53] [Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR): Cloud Workload Conformal Prediction for Virtual Machines in Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24773)
*Mehryar Majd,Feng Cheng,Ali Pahlevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自助法共形预测（Bootstrapping Conformal Prediction）的数据驱动预测区间构建方法，用于云环境中虚拟机（VM）的合理大小调整推荐（RSR），以提升资源分配效率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VM分配与调度方法未能有效应对VM利用率的波动性和不可预测性，导致资源过度或不足配置，造成成本浪费和性能下降。需要一种能够准确捕捉云资源需求不确定性的高质量区间预测方法。

Method: 采用自助法共形预测（Bootstrapping Conformal Prediction）构建预测区间（PIs）；通过AI/ML回归技术学习工作负载利用模式、识别多时间序列间的相关性、预测中到长期利用趋势；利用回测（Backtesting）评估模型表现。

Result: AI驱动的ML回归模型在云资源利用率预测上取得了有前景的结果；通过模型排名识别出适用于长生命周期VM候选的最佳方法；所提框架增强了合理大小调整推荐的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架能够支持在动态云环境中进行更具成本效益的资源分配，为超大规模云提供商的数据驱动型合理大小调整推荐提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Managing cloud infrastructure efficiently, especially in environments of large cloud providers or hyperscalers, requires optimizing the use of physical resources to minimize costs and maximize performance. Selecting the right virtual machine (VM) sizes is crucial to achieving cost efficiency in these dynamic environments. However, traditional VM allocation and scheduling approaches often fail to account for the fluctuating and unpredictable nature of VM utilization, leading to inefficiencies such as over- or under-provisioning of resources. High-quality interval prediction helps accurately capture uncertainty in cloud resource demand and supports cloud operators in efficient instance provisioning.
  As an effective and reliable framework for constructing prediction intervals (PIs), conformal prediction (CP) is used for mid- and long-term forecasting tasks in cloud computing environments. This study proposes a new data-driven PI construction approach using bootstrapping conformal prediction for modern, dynamic, data-driven Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR) to enhance provisioning for diverse application workloads on hyperscalers. By learning workload utilization patterns, identifying correlations across multiple time series, and predicting medium- to long-term utilization trends, this research seeks to improve the efficiency of cloud and data center operations through an AI/ML-based provisioning pipeline.
  Our study demonstrates that AI-driven models, powered by machine learning regression techniques and evaluated using backtesting, achieve promising forecasting results for cloud resource utilization. Additionally, we rank the selected models to identify top-performing approaches for long-life VM candidates. The proposed framework enhances right-sizing recommendations and supports more cost-effective resource allocation in dynamic cloud environments.

</details>


### [54] [Atmospheric Diffusion-Guided Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Nuclear Radiation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24774)
*Tengfei Lyu,Jindong Han,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NRFormer+，一种用于全国核辐射预测的时空Transformer模型，通过非平稳时间注意力、密度自适应空间注意力和大气扩散模块，在13个基线方法上取得了最佳性能，突发变化MAE最高降低19.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核辐射对公共健康和环境构成持续威胁，福岛事故后排污水进一步加剧了担忧。现有监测网络虽能记录大量辐射和气象数据，但因时间序列非平稳、监测站空间分布极不均匀（日本78%的站点集中在不到6%的区域）、辐射与气象因素的复杂耦合关系，使得数据驱动的可靠预测极具挑战。

Method: NRFormer+采用时空Transformer架构，包含三个核心模块：(1) 非平稳时间注意力机制，处理高度非平稳的时间序列；(2) 密度自适应空间注意力机制，应对监测站分布不均匀问题；(3) 大气扩散模块，基于物理模型估计气象条件驱动辐射扩散的过程，将物理信号作为架构先验注入网络。

Result: 在两个数据集上，NRFormer+在全部13个基线方法中取得最优性能，突发变化MAE相比最强基线降低最多19.1%，推理延迟相当。

Conclusion: NRFormer+有效融合了物理先验与数据驱动方法，解决了核辐射预测中时间非平稳性、空间不均匀性和气象耦合三大挑战，为全国范围核辐射预测提供了新的解决方案。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Nuclear radiation, the energy released during atomic decay, poses persistent risks to public health and the environment, and concerns have only grown since the Fukushima accident and the recent commencement of treated-water discharge. Modern monitoring networks now record radiation levels and accompanying weather conditions at thousands of stations, opening the door to nationwide forecasting that can inform emergency response, agricultural advisories, and routine public-safety decisions. However, turning this abundance of monitoring data into reliable forecasts is difficult for three reasons. First, the time series at each station are highly non-stationary, shaped by radioactive decay, weather variability, and irregular human interventions. Second, monitoring stations are severely unevenly distributed in space. Roughly 78% of Japan's stations sit in less than 6% of the country, clustered near Fukushima, which breaks the assumptions of standard graph-based models. Third, radiation co-evolves with heterogeneous context such as wind, temperature, and humidity through atmospheric transport processes that purely data-driven models struggle to capture from observations alone. In this study, we introduce NRFormer+, a spatio-temporal Transformer for nationwide nuclear radiation forecasting. NRFormer+ couples non-stationary temporal attention and density-adaptive spatial attention with a new atmospheric diffusion module that estimates how meteorology drives radiation dispersion and injects this physical signal into the network as an architectural prior. NRFormer+ delivers state-of-the-art accuracy on both datasets across all 13 baselines, reducing sudden-change MAE by up to 19.1% over the strongest baseline at comparable inference latency. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/tfeilyu/NRFormer_Plus.

</details>


### [55] [Steering topology distributions for unified generative design of architected metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24777)
*Haolin Li,Yuyang Miao,Menglei Li,Jinshuai Bai,Liyuan Wang,Xin Liu,Bo Gao,Jiantao Liu,Danilo Mandic,Zahra Sharif Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi,Weiqiu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式拓扑优化（GenTO）框架，通过预训练扩散模型学习拓扑先验，再利用用户定义的物理目标和约束将拓扑分布引导至高性能区域，实现跨不同设计任务的可重用拓扑优化，在热极端化、多目标形态控制、负泊松比设计和振动传输等任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑设计方法通常针对单个设计问题定制，难以在目标、约束和物理功能变化时有效且广泛地应用拓扑知识。

Method: GenTO框架包含两个阶段：首先在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型以学习拓扑先验；然后通过用户定义的物理目标和约束，迭代地将拓扑分布引导至任务特定的高性能区域，将优化对象从单一结构转变为任务自适应的拓扑分布。

Result: 在热极端化、多目标形态控制、目标属性辅助设计（负泊松比）和振动传输设计等多种拓扑问题中，GenTO可重用预训练拓扑先验、保持结构多样性，并通过数值基准和实验验证达到高性能解。

Conclusion: 可重用拓扑知识可作为有效且可扩展的架构化超材料设计的统一原则。

Abstract: Architected metamaterials derive their functions from structure, creating vast opportunities to program physical responses through topology design. However, existing design methods are often tailored to individual design problems, making limited use of topology knowledge for effective and broadly applicable design as objectives, constraints, and physical functions change. Here we introduce Generative Topology Optimization (GenTO), a unified framework that turns a learned topology prior into a reusable design engine. GenTO trains a diffusion model on a large full-order topology dataset and then iteratively steers the resulting topology distribution toward task-specific high-performing regions using user-defined physical objectives and constraints. This shifts the object of optimization from a single structure to a task-adapted topology distribution. Across topology design problems spanning thermal extremization, multi-objective morphology control, property-targeted auxetic design, and vibration transmission design, GenTO reuses pretrained topology priors for heterogeneous tasks, preserves structural diversity, and reaches high-performing solutions supported by numerical benchmarks and experimental validation. These results establish reusable topology knowledge as a unified principle for effective and scalable architected metamaterial design.

</details>


### [56] [HOBA: Hierarchical On-Policy Bidding Agents for Adaptive Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24779)
*Ji Wu,Yunshan Peng,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Wenzheng Shu,Jinan Pang,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HOBA（分层在线竞价智能体）提出了一种分层强化学习框架，通过LLM推理超参数、SARSA选择专家模型、动态专家池执行出价，解决了在线广告竞价系统中模型缺乏在线适应性和依赖人工调参的问题，在AuctionNet和线上A/B测试中取得一致性提升，目标成本提升3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线广告竞价系统部署了多个离线训练专家模型（如PID、MPC、离线RL策略），但存在两大关键局限：1）缺乏对非平稳拍卖市场的在线适应性；2）依赖昂贵的人工超参数调优（如出价边界、预算节奏约束）。

Method: HOBA采用三层时间尺度的分层强化学习框架：高层用LLM通过思考-行动-观察-反思循环和历史经验检索从上下文信号推断超参数；中层用SARSA智能体动态选择专家模型并引入因果调整消除选择偏差；底层由动态专家池（PID、MPC、IQL、Decision Transformer）在高层约束下执行出价。在线学习限定在离散的专家选择而非连续的出价优化空间，降低探索风险。

Result: 在AuctionNet基准和大规模线上A/B测试中均取得一致性改进，线上部署实现目标成本提升+3.6%，带来显著商业价值。

Conclusion: HOBA通过分层多智能体竞价范式，有效结合了LLM的上下文推理、在线强化学习的适应性以及离线专家模型的稳定性，为在线广告竞价系统提供了一种可部署的在线自适应方案。

Abstract: Online advertising bidding systems typically deploy multiple offline-trained expert models (e.g., PID controllers, model predictive control, offline RL policies) but face two critical limitations: lack of online adaptability to non-stationary auction markets, and reliance on costly manual tuning of hyperparameters such as bid bounds and budget pacing constraints. We propose HOBA (Hierarchical On-policy Bidding Agents), a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that decouples strategic reasoning, model selection, and bid execution across three time scales. At the high level, a large language model infers hyperparameters from contextual signals through a Think-Act-Observe-Reflect loop with historical experience retrieval. At the mid level, a SARSA agent dynamically selects among expert models, incorporating causal adjustment to eliminate selection bias. At the low level, a dynamic expert pool (PID, MPC, IQL, Decision Transformer) executes bids under high-level constraints. This design confines online learning to discrete expert selection rather than continuous bid optimization, significantly reducing exploration risk while maintaining adaptability. Experiments on the AuctionNet benchmark and a large-scale A/B test demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. In a large-scale online deployment, HOBA delivered substantial business value, achieving a +3.6\% increase in target cost, proving the effectiveness of our hierarchical multi-agent bidding paradigm.

</details>


### [57] [LivingArena: Do LLMs Know What Other LLMs Don't? Peer-Probing as Scalable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24780)
*Xingyu Chen,Rui Wang,Zhaopeng Tu,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingArena是一个自动化、抗污染的评估框架，通过让LLMs互相提问来探测对方知识边界，从而生成稳定的Elo排行榜，并为模型提供可扩展的低成本持续评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准测试存在数据污染和饱和问题，无法区分顶尖模型；人类偏好具有主观性。作者希望通过利用LLMs相互识别知识边界的能力，构建一种动态评估方法来克服这些局限。

Method: LivingArena框架中，模型轮流充当提问者和回答者。提问者需主动探测对手的知识盲区，若回答者答错则提问者获得奖励；反之回答者得分。由强模型组成的评审团验证问题答案的客观性，未通过则惩罚提问者。采用Elo评分系统生成排行榜。

Result: 对10个前沿LLM的评估产生了稳定的Elo排行榜。行为分析表明模型能够识别并利用对手的认知边界，自对弈和锦标赛日志显示模型会定位并在对手薄弱维度上加强攻击。

Conclusion: 同行探测不仅测量静态知识回忆，还评估事实严谨性和探测对手弱点的高阶能力。该方法与人类偏好相关性弱，为持续评估提供了一个可扩展的低成本方案。

Abstract: Evaluating frontier LLMs is challenging: static benchmarks suffer from contamination and saturation -- leaving users unable to distinguish top models and developers blind to specific failure modes -- while human preference is subjective. In this paper, our question is: \emph{Do LLMs know what other LLMs don't? And can we leverage this dynamic for evaluation?} We present \textbf{LivingArena}, an automated, contamination-resistant evaluation framework. In this framework, models take turns proposing questions, aiming to pose items that opponents cannot answer correctly. Questioners are encouraged to actively identify and exploit opponents' knowledge boundaries, receiving rewards when the answerer fails, while the answerer is rewarded otherwise. To ensure questions contain objectively verifiable answers, a judge panel of strong models validates them, penalizing questioners if validation fails. Evaluating ten frontier LLMs, LivingArena yields a stable Elo leaderboard. Our behavioral analyses show that models identify and exploit their peers' cognitive boundaries: self-play and tournament logs indicate that they localize and double down on opponents' weak dimensions. Beyond static knowledge recall, peer probing measures factual rigor and the higher-order ability to probe an opponent's weaknesses, correlating only weakly with human preference and offering a scalable, low-cost approach to continuous evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Personalization, Personas, and Forecasting in Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24782)
*James Wedgwood,Pratiksha Thaker,Neil Kale,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了四种大语言模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-235B）在101道世界价值观调查（WVS）问题上，不同提示框架（用户适配、角色扮演、第三人称预测）对文化价值观对齐的影响。结果显示，提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素，第三人称预测对齐效果最好，而个性化与角色扮演较弱且不稳定。对齐提升集中在宗教、性别角色、物质价值观等维度，制度信任和民主相关问题仍难以对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM行为在不同人类身份框架（用户适配、角色扮演、第三人称预测）下是否可互换，以及提示框架如何影响模型与人类文化价值观的对齐程度。

Method: 使用101道WVS衍生问题，覆盖13个语言-国家切片，评估GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-235B四种模型。比较纯语言基线、用户国家、角色国家、第三人称四种提示框架，共收集21,008条模型回复数据。

Result: 提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素；第三人称预测对三款模型的方向对齐最优，个性化与角色扮演较弱；对齐提升集中在宗教、性别角色、物质价值观等维度，制度信任和民主相关问题仍难以对齐。

Conclusion: 提示框架并非文化价值观抽取中的装饰性选择，而是会从根本上改变模型行为和对齐度量结果。

Abstract: LLM behavior may be conditioned by human identity in several ways: they may be asked to adapt to users, role-play populations, or forecast how people would answer value-laden questions. We test whether these framings are interchangeable using the World Values Survey (WVS). We evaluate GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Qwen3-235B on 101 WVS-derived questions across 13 language-country slices, comparing a language-only baseline with user-country, persona-country, and third-person prompts. Across 21,008 model-response rows, prompt framing is a first-order determinant of cultural alignment: country cues often shift answers substantially, but not all shifts move toward matched human response distributions. Third-person forecasting yields the strongest directional alignment for three of the four hosted models, while personalization and role-play are weaker or less stable. Alignment gains concentrate on salient value dimensions such as religiosity, gender roles, and work-oriented material values, whereas institutional trust and democracy-related questions remain difficult. These results show that prompt framing is not a cosmetic choice in cultural value elicitation; it changes both model behavior and measured alignment.

</details>


### [59] [Unified Semantic Modeling Framework for Large-Scale Job Understanding at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24783)
*Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Jianqiang Shen,Qi Xiao,Benjamin Hoan Le,Wen Pu,Saurabh Gupta,Ran Zhou,Neha Saraf,Alice Leung,Qianqi Shen,Liangjie Hong,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的统一语义建模框架，通过微调开源SLM并设计合成任务与推理轨迹，结合多适配器架构和属性分组，实现了对非结构化招聘帖文的标准化属性提取与分类，在零样本泛化、性能提升和运营复杂度降低方面取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn需要将非结构化的招聘帖文转化为标准化的岗位属性，以支持人才与机会的精准匹配。然而，现有系统在规模化、成本效率和性能上存在挑战，亟需一个统一、高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 首先对开源小语言模型进行微调，使用精心设计的合成任务和推理轨迹增强训练数据；任务涵盖基于分类法的分类和与分类无关的实体提取。在此基础上，采用多适配器架构（multi-adapter architecture）结合属性分组（attribute grouping），实现高效的任务特定适配和简化的模型管理。

Result: 离线评估和在线A/B测试均表明，该方法在显著提升性能的同时，降低了运营复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建工业级文本理解系统提供了实用经验，证明了小语言模型结合合成数据与多适配器架构在规模化场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Job understanding is critical to LinkedIn's mission of connecting talent with opportunity. This task involves transforming unstructured and noisy job postings into standardized or derived job attributes that power numerous LinkedIn products. However, building a scalable, cost-efficient, and high-performing job understanding system remains challenging. In this paper, we present a unified semantic modeling framework powered by a small language model (SLM) to address the challenges. We begin by fine-tuning an open-source SLM using a suite of carefully curated synthetic tasks augmented with reasoning traces. These tasks jointly target taxonomy-guided classification and taxonomy-agnostic entity extraction. This allows the resulting model to acquire robust zero-shot generalization for job understanding in structured and unstructured contexts. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a multi-adapter architecture with attribute grouping to facilitate efficient task-specific adaptation while streamlining model management across diverse downstream attributes. Offline evaluations and online A/B tests demonstrate significant performance improvement while reducing operational complexity. Our work provides practical insights into building industry-scale text understanding systems.

</details>


### [60] [On the Use of LLMs for Specialised Terminology: A Good Alternative to Corpora?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24784)
*Joachim Minder,Guillaume Wisniewski,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLMs在专业翻译（英译法）中查找术语等价物的能力，比较了四种模型、两种提示策略和两个专业领域。结果显示Claude Sonnet 4.5最优，DeepSeek最稳定，但LLMs目前仍无法替代专业语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业翻译依赖语料库等术语资源，但这些资源的编制和利用存在耗时长、技术要求高、数据获取困难等局限性。因此研究LLMs能否帮助专业译员在英译法中查找术语等价物。

Method: 基于地球环境与行星科学（EEPS）和自然语言处理（NLP）两个专业领域，各选80个术语，比较GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5和DeepSeek四种模型，采用术语模式和翻译模式两种提示策略。

Result: 不同模型、提示策略及（程度较轻的）领域之间存在明显差异。Claude Sonnet 4.5在最有利配置下表现最佳，DeepSeek稳定性最强。置信度估计仅为术语准确性的部分指标。

Conclusion: LLMs可作为专业翻译的有用工具，但当前阶段无法替代专业语料库。该研究为未来在工作和教育环境中探索LLMs对专业译员的实际效用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Specialised translation relies on the use of documentary and terminological resources, including corpora. These resources are particularly useful for terminology. However, their compilation and exploitation have several limitations: they require time, technical skills and access to data that can be difficult to collect. This study examines the extent to which LLMs can assist specialised translators in finding equivalents from English to French. We evaluate four proprietary models, GPT-4o, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and DeepSeek, in two specialised domains, Earth, Environmental and Planetary Sciences (EEPS) and Natural Language Processing (NLP). The experiment is based on 80 terms per domain and compares two prompting strategies: a terminology and a translation mode. The results highlight clear differences between models, prompting strategies and, to a lesser extent, domains. Claude Sonnet 4.5 achieves the best results in the most favourable configuration, while DeepSeek stands out for its greater stability. Analysis of confidence estimates also shows that they are only a partial indicator of terminological accuracy. Overall, the findings suggest that LLMs can be useful tools for specialised translators, but cannot, at this stage, replace specialised corpora. This research therefore paves the way for future work on the real practical usefulness of LLMs for specialised translators in work and educational contexts.

</details>


### [61] [SpecPrefetch: Parameter-Efficient Expert Prefetching for Sparse MoE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24787)
*Jinwei Kong,Runqi Meng,Fanyi Wang,Wentao Qiu,Haotian Hu,Yongjian Zhou,Zhenhua Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SpecPrefetch，一种用于稀疏MoE模型推理的参数高效预取框架。通过轻量级适配器预测下一层专家候选进行异步传输，同时保持原生路由器决定最终执行专家，从而解开路由依赖的传输瓶颈，在受限内存设备上实现最高20%的解码吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE模型在有限加速器内存下难以部署全部专家池。专家卸载虽能缓解内存压力，但引入了路由依赖的传输瓶颈：原生top-K路由串行化了路由、专家加载和专家执行过程，导致推理延迟增加。

Method: 提出SpecPrefetch框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 共享轻量级适配器用于预测下一层专家候选（仅用于异步传输）；(2) 冻结的原生路由器仍决定最终执行专家，分离传输预测与执行路由；(3) 窗口感知调度器在缓存和带宽约束下优先处理可行传输。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B和DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny上，10个模型-基准设置中有9个达到最佳平均专家召回率，且可训练参数远少于基线。在Snapdragon 8 Elite设备上，相比计算优化的卸载运行时，解码吞吐量提升最高达20%。

Conclusion: SpecPrefetch通过分离传输预测与执行路由有效解决了卸载MoE推理中的路由依赖瓶颈，预测错误仅影响传输效率而非模型输出，在存储受限的MoE部署中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models expand foundation model capacity through conditional expert activation, but their full expert pools remain difficult to deploy under limited accelerator memory. Although expert offloading alleviates memory pressure by moving inactive experts to host memory or storage, it introduces a routing-dependent transfer bottleneck: required experts are known only after native top-\(K\) routing, which serializes routing, expert loading, and expert execution during inference. To address this bottleneck, we propose SpecPrefetch, a parameter-efficient prefetching framework for offloaded MoE inference. SpecPrefetch uses a shared lightweight adapter to predict next-layer expert candidates only for asynchronous transfer, while the frozen native router still determines the final executed experts. By separating transfer prediction from execution routing, SpecPrefetch reduces exposed expert-loading latency without changing pretrained routing semantics, so prediction errors affect transfer efficiency rather than model outputs. In addition, a window-aware scheduler prioritizes feasible transfers under cache and bandwidth constraints. Across Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B and DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny, SpecPrefetch achieves the best average expert recall in 9 out of 10 model-benchmark settings with substantially fewer trainable parameters than learned predictor baselines. On a Snapdragon 8 Elite device, SpecPrefetch further improves decoding throughput by up to \(20\%\) over a compute-optimized offloading runtime, demonstrating practical benefits for storage-constrained MoE deployment. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/wei390/SpecPrefetch.

</details>


### [62] [GLIDE: Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24788)
*Vimal William,Ravi Tandon,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIDE提出了一种引导式逐层混合注意力机制，通过逐层自适应地结合滑动窗口softmax注意力与线性循环聚合，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低长上下文解码中的KV缓存I/O和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型处理长上下文时，KV缓存的显存I/O和计算开销成为解码阶段的主要吞吐量瓶颈，亟需高效的注意力机制优化方案。

Method: 利用逐层异质性观察：浅层对softmax移除敏感故保留可变形softmax窗口，深层存在冗余可被线性循环替代。引入逐层自适应机制，为每层平衡线性循环与可变大小softmax窗口，非均匀压缩softmax计算足迹。

Result: 在长上下文生成中实现端到端延迟降低，同时不损害生成质量，取得了优越的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 逐层非均匀的混合注意力设计能够有效解决长上下文场景下KV缓存效率问题，为高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: As Large Language Models scale to increasingly long contexts, the memory I/O and computational overhead of the Key-Value (KV) cache during decoding emerges as the primary throughput bottleneck. To address this, we propose GLIDE, a Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention that strategically integrates sliding-window softmax attention with linear recurrent aggregation. GLIDE is motivated by layer-wise heterogeneity: early layers exhibit high sensitivity to softmax removal, while deeper layers demonstrate redundancy and tolerate aggressive replacement by linear alternatives. Leveraging this insight, GLIDE introduces a layer-wise adaptive mechanism wherein each layer balances an efficient linear recurrence with a variable-sized softmax window.
